# Card, FAQ and ruling traceability

Generated by `gcg-diff traceability --write` (docs/port/PLAN.md §9.2) from the card database,
the gcg-api FAQ and ruling lists, the N/A reasons in `tests/meta/`, the Python tags
(`port/corpus/v1/tier1/tests/tags.json`), the real-engine replay of the per-test traces and
the `// gcg-tags:` lines of native Rust tests. A non-vanilla card also counts for Rust when the
executed-program census saw it run and every census game trace passes (`gcg-diff coverage`).

Summary: cards 1152 real (840 non-vanilla, 840 with Rust evidence; 312 vanilla); FAQ 119 (0 N/A, 119 with Rust evidence); rulings 368 (8 N/A, 360 with Rust evidence).
